C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

血小板减少症和免疫性血小板减少症(ITP)的背景、风险因素及与疫苗接种的关联:一项范围综述。

Incidence, risk factors, and association with vaccination of thrombocytopenia and immune thrombocytopenia (ITP): A scoping review.
作者： Matthew Z Dudley, Hammad Ali, Marta Rojo Villaescusa, Barbara Law
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估血小板减少症和免疫性血小板减少症(ITP)的背景、风险因素及其与疫苗接种的关联,以填补对疫苗相关安全特征的理解空白。Brighton Collaboration于2007年开发了血小板减少症的统一病例定义,以提高疫苗安全证据的收集质量,并实现研究间的有效比较。
设计与对象
进行了一项范围综述,以总结当前关于血小板减少症和ITP的证据,包括其背景、风险因素以及与疫苗的关联。综述涵盖了来自不同地区和年龄组的研究,数据收集基于2024年的搜索。
核心方法
2024 年 7 月进行了两次 PubMed 检索,以确定关于背景 发病率 和非 疫苗 风险因素以及 疫苗 关联的研究。提取相关数据并将其整合到配套指南表中。
主要结果
Twenty-two articles contributed to the updated summary of background incidence, 36 to non-vaccine risk factors, and 93 to vaccine associations. Background incidence estimates of thrombocytopenia ranged from 1.6 to 92.1 per 100,000 person-years, depending on the study, region, and age range.
解释与创新
研究结果强调,虽然血小板减少症有许多危险因素,包括 疫苗接种 与 麻疹 相比,但 疫苗 与 ITP 的关联性不一致,含有 麻疹 的 疫苗 显示风险增加,而其他 疫苗 显示没有显着关联。
科研与公卫意义
结果可以为 监测 和血小板减少症的风险评估工作提供信息,特别是评估免疫接种后的不良事件,并可能指导未来关于 疫苗 安全性的研究。
局限与证据强度
证据基于范围界定审查,该审查可能在捕获所有相关研究方面存在局限性,并且研究结果受到不同人群和地区 发病率 比率的变化的影响。证据强度中等。

Incidence, risk factors, and association with vaccination of thrombocytopenia and immune thrombocytopenia (ITP): A scoping review.

Authors: Matthew Z Dudley, Hammad Ali, Marta Rojo Villaescusa, Barbara Law
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The Brighton Collaboration published its standard harmonized case definition for thrombocytopenia in 2007 to facilitate collection of valid and reproducible vaccine safety evidence and enable valid comparisons across studies. A companion guide to this case definition (including background incidence, risk factors, evidence for or against an association with vaccination, medical codes, data collection forms, and algorithms to support level of diagnostic certainty assessment) was initially published in 2021 and updated in 2025 based on a formal scoping review. This article reports the results of this scoping review, summarizing current evidence regarding the background incidence of and risk factors for thrombocytopenia, including immune thrombocytopenia (ITP) and its potential association with vaccination. METHODS: Two PubMed searches were conducted in July 2024: one for background incidence and non-vaccine risk factors, and one for vaccine associations. Search results were screened and relevant data were extracted and added to the corresponding companion guide tables. FINDINGS: Twenty-two articles contributed to the updated summary of background incidence, 36 articles contributed to the updated summary of non-vaccine risk factors, and 93 articles contributed to the updated summary of vaccine associations. Estimates of the background incidence of thrombocytopenia range between 1.6 and 92.1 per 100,000 person-years, depending on the study, region, and age range. Non-vaccine risk factors for thrombocytopenia include age, sex, genetics, season, geography, comorbidities, iatrogenesis, infection, medication, and food. While studies have consistently found an increased risk of ITP with measles-containing vaccines, and multiple studies found an increased risk following adenoviral COVID-19 vaccination, studies of other vaccines (including mRNA and inactivated COVID-19 vaccines) have largely found no association. CONCLUSION: Though rare, thrombocytopenia has many risk factors, including vaccination against measles. This review should be useful to stakeholders attempting to assess the occurrence of thrombocytopenia across a variety of settings, including as an adverse event following immunization.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess the incidence, risk factors, and association with vaccination of thrombocytopenia and immune thrombocytopenia (ITP), addressing a gap in understanding vaccine-related safety profiles. The Brighton Collaboration developed a harmonized case definition for thrombocytopenia in 2007 to improve vaccine safety evidence collection and enable valid cross-study comparisons.
Design and population
A scoping review was conducted to summarize current evidence regarding thrombocytopenia and ITP, including their background incidence and risk factors, with a focus on vaccine associations. The review included studies from various regions and age groups, with data collected from 2024 searches.
Core methods
Two PubMed searches were performed in July 2024 to identify studies on background incidence and non-vaccine risk factors, as well as vaccine associations. Relevant data were extracted and integrated into the companion guide tables.
Main results
Twenty-two articles contributed to the updated summary of background incidence, 36 to non-vaccine risk factors, and 93 to vaccine associations. Background incidence estimates of thrombocytopenia ranged from 1.6 to 92.1 per 100,000 person-years, depending on the study, region, and age range.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight that while thrombocytopenia has many risk factors, including vaccination against measles, vaccine associations with ITP are inconsistent, with measles-containing vaccines showing increased risk and other vaccines showing no significant association.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform surveillance and risk assessment efforts for thrombocytopenia, particularly in evaluating adverse events following immunization, and may guide future studies on vaccine safety.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on a scoping review, which may have limitations in capturing all relevant studies, and the findings are subject to the variability of incidence rates across different populations and regions. The evidence strength is moderate.

阿富汗 24-35 个月儿童国家儿童免疫计划的完成情况及相关因素:2022-23 年 MICS 全国分析。

Completion of the National Childhood Immunization Schedule and Associated Factors Among Children Aged 24-35 Months in Afghanistan: A Nationwide Analysis of MICS 2022-23.
作者： Omid Dadras, Muhammad Haroon Stanikzai, Essa Tawfiq, Massoma Jafari
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
阿富汗的儿童免疫覆盖率仍然较低,而且关于第一年之后更广泛的国家计划的完成情况的证据有限,这造成了知识差距,本研究通过估计完整计划覆盖率并检查与 24-35 个月儿童完成情况相关的因素来解决这一问题。
设计与对象
该研究对 2022-23 年阿富汗多指标类集调查的全国代表性数据进行了横断面分析,覆盖全国 6342 名 24-35 个月的儿童。
核心方法
调查加权分析估计了 ​​疫苗 特定覆盖率和系列内辍学率,并且预先指定的多变量逻辑回归模型确定了与完整进度完成相关的因素。
主要结果
只有 16.2% (95% CI 14.7-17.7) 的儿童完成了全部时间表,IPV2 (24.6%) 和含有 麻疹 的 疫苗 剂量 2 的覆盖率较低 (36.8%),接受 IPV1 的儿童中有 49.2% 缺少 IPV2,省级完成率从 3.1% 到 37.7% 不等。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,国家儿童免疫接种计划的完成率仍然很低,多剂量系列的人员流失量很大,而且地域差异明显。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,改善 疫苗接种 的连续性、后续剂量的随访以及以家庭为中心的 疫苗接种 沟通可能有助于改善时间表的完成并解决观察到的地理差异。
局限与证据强度
证据基于具有全国代表性的大型样本(A3),但仅限于调查数据,引入了潜在的回忆偏差和不答复,从而降低了置信度;总体证据强度中等。

Completion of the National Childhood Immunization Schedule and Associated Factors Among Children Aged 24-35 Months in Afghanistan: A Nationwide Analysis of MICS 2022-23.

Authors: Omid Dadras, Muhammad Haroon Stanikzai, Essa Tawfiq, Massoma Jafari
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Childhood immunization coverage remains low in Afghanistan, but evidence is limited regarding completion of the broader national immunization schedule beyond the first year of life. This study estimated full-schedule immunization coverage and examined factors associated with its completion among Afghan children aged 24-35 months. METHODS: We analysed nationally representative data from the Afghanistan Multiple Indicator Cluster Survey (MICS) 2022-23, including 6342 children aged 24-35 months. Full immunization was defined according to the MICS TC.11b indicator for completion of all vaccinations included in the national childhood immunization schedule. Survey-weighted analyses estimated vaccine-specific coverage, within-series dropout and provincial variation. A prespecified survey-weighted multivariable logistic regression model was used to identify factors associated with full-schedule completion. RESULTS: Only 16.2% (95% CI 14.7-17.7) of children had completed the full national immunization schedule. Coverage was particularly low for Inactivated Poliovirus Vaccine (IPV2) (24.6%) and measles-containing vaccine (MCV)- 2 (36.8%), and 49.2% of children who had received IPV1 had not received IPV2. Full-schedule completion varied markedly across provinces, from 3.1 to 37.7%. In the adjusted analysis, maternal primary education (AOR 1.55, 95% CI 1.11-2.19) and secondary or higher education (AOR 1.72, 95% CI 1.15-2.57), paternal primary education (AOR 1.68, 95% CI 1.13-2.50) and secondary or higher education (AOR 1.94, 95% CI 1.48-2.55), and maternal mobile-phone ownership (AOR 1.29, 95% CI 1.03-1.61) were associated with greater odds of full-schedule completion. CONCLUSION: Completion of the national childhood immunization schedule remains very low in Afghanistan, with substantial attrition across multi-dose vaccine series and marked geographical variation. Strengthening continuity of vaccination, follow-up for later doses, and accessible family-focused vaccination communication may help improve schedule completion.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Childhood immunization coverage remains low in Afghanistan and evidence is limited regarding completion of the broader national schedule beyond the first year, creating a knowledge gap that this study addresses by estimating full-schedule coverage and examining factors associated with completion among children aged 24-35 months.
Design and population
The study employed a cross-sectional analysis of nationally representative data from the Afghanistan Multiple Indicator Cluster Survey 2022-23, covering 6342 children aged 24-35 months across the country.
Core methods
Survey-weighted analyses estimated vaccine-specific coverage and within-series dropout, and a prespecified multivariable logistic regression model identified factors associated with full-schedule completion.
Main results
Only 16.2% (95% CI 14.7-17.7) of children completed the full schedule, with low coverage for IPV2 (24.6%) and measles-containing vaccine dose 2 (36.8%), 49.2% of children who received IPV1 missing IPV2, and provincial completion ranging from 3.1% to 37.7%.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that completion of the national childhood immunization schedule remains very low, with substantial attrition across multi-dose series and marked geographical variation.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that improving continuity of vaccination, follow-up for later doses, and family-focused vaccination communication may help improve schedule completion and address the observed geographic disparities.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on a large nationally representative sample (A3) but is limited to survey data, introducing potential recall bias and non-response, which reduces confidence; overall evidence strength is moderate.

不让任何人掉队:使用世卫组织/联合国儿童基金会免疫联合报告表中的数据对儿童 疫苗接种 及时性和追赶情况进行监测的全球分析

Leave no one behind: Global Analysis on monitoring childhood vaccination timeliness and catch-up using data from the WHO/UNICEF joint reporting form on Immunization
作者： M. Carolina Danovaro‐Holliday, Ignacio Castro-Aguirre, Mitsuki Koh, I. Kim, Yoann Nedelec, Marcela Contreras, Naor Bar‐Zeev, Martha Velandia-González, Marta Gacic-Dobo, Danovaro-Holliday MC
摘要中文翻译在 Covid-19 大流行之后的几年中,免疫计划越来越关注执行和监测 疫苗接种 补种的能力(即晚于建议时间表施用的剂量)。然而,目前尚不清楚信息系统是否能够监控延迟的疫苗接种。我们分析了来自 194 个国家(2016-2022 年)的 WHO/UNICEF 联合报告表数据,以了解追踪延迟儿童 疫苗接种 的系统是否可用,并量化含 麻疹 的 疫苗 (MCV1) 第一剂的追赶剂量。对于拉丁美洲和加勒比国家,我们还探讨了第三剂白喉-破伤风-百日咳 疫苗 (DTP3) 的数据。我们评估了报告的完整性和一致性、延迟剂量的比例以及考虑及时和延迟给药的 疫苗接种 覆盖范围。分析按世卫组织区域、世界银行收入分类和全球疫苗和免疫联盟支持资格进行分类。结果显示,不同地区和收入群体的国家捕获延迟剂量的能力存在很大差异。2016 年至 2022 年间,每年报告有记录延迟剂量系统的国家比例为 55.5% 至 64.4%,其中美洲地区 (AMR) 报告的比例始终较高 (77.1%–82.9%)。在有可用数据的国家中,延迟 MCV1 剂量的中位数比例为 4.4%,这表明在 队列研究 监测中纳入追赶剂量将使报告的覆盖率增加约 4 个百分点。与不符合条件的国家相比,符合全球疫苗免疫联盟条件的国家的增幅较小。该研究总结了监测追赶疫苗接种的信息系统的全球差异,介绍了队列研究覆盖率监测的例子,并说明需要进一步完善免疫信息系统,并探索通过追赶活动提高疫苗接种覆盖率和人群免疫力的潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决 Covid-19 大流行后对 麻疹 追赶的日益关注,并评估免疫计划监测含有 麻疹 的 疫苗 延迟剂量的能力。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 2016 年至 2022 年间来自 194 个国家的世卫组织/联合国儿童基金会联合报告表数据,重点关注追踪儿童 疫苗接种 延迟的系统的可用性,并量化含有 麻疹 的 疫苗 (MCV1) 第一剂的追赶剂量。
核心方法
研究人员评估了报告系统的完整性和一致性、延迟剂量的比例和 疫苗接种 覆盖范围,同时考虑及时和延迟给药,并按世卫组织区域、世界银行收入分类和全球疫苗和免疫联盟支持资格分析数据。
主要结果
结果显示,不同地区和收入群体的国家捕获延迟剂量的能力存在很大差异,美洲地区报告称拥有每年记录延迟剂量系统的国家比例较高(77.1%–82.9%)。在有可用数据的国家中,延迟接种 MCV1 剂量的中位数比例为 4.4%,这表明如果包括追赶剂量,报告的覆盖率将增加约 4 个百分点。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调需要改进免疫信息系统,以更好地捕获延迟剂量,并探索通过追赶活动增加 疫苗接种 覆盖率和群体免疫力的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为加强全球 监测 和儿童 疫苗接种 及时性监测的努力提供信息,特别是在追踪延迟剂量系统不太发达的地区和收入群体。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于免疫计划的自我报告数据,这可能会引入偏差或不一致,并且由于分析仅具有抽象性质,因此证据被认为是中等的。

Leave no one behind: Global Analysis on monitoring childhood vaccination timeliness and catch-up using data from the WHO/UNICEF joint reporting form on Immunization

Authors: M. Carolina Danovaro‐Holliday, Ignacio Castro-Aguirre, Mitsuki Koh, I. Kim, Yoann Nedelec, Marcela Contreras, Naor Bar‐Zeev, Martha Velandia-González, Marta Gacic-Dobo, Danovaro-Holliday MC
Original abstract (English)
In the years following the Covid-19 pandemic, immunization programmes have increasingly focused on the capacity to conduct and monitor catch-up vaccination—for doses administered later than the recommended schedule. However, it is unclear whether information systems are able to monitor delayed vaccination. We analyzed WHO/UNICEF Joint Reporting Form data from 194 countries (2016–2022) to understand if systems for tracking delayed childhood vaccination are available and quantify catch-up doses of the first dose of a measles-containing vaccine (MCV1). For Latin American and Caribbean countries, we also explored data for the third dose of diphtheria-tetanus-pertussis vaccine (DTP3). We assessed the completeness and consistency of reporting, the proportion of delayed doses, and vaccination coverage considering both timely and delayed administration. Analyses were disaggregated by WHO Region, World Bank income classification, and Gavi support eligibility. Results show substantial variability across regions and income groups in countries’ ability to capture delayed doses. Between 2016 and 2022, the proportion of countries reporting a system to record delayed doses ranged from 55.5% to 64.4% annually, with the Region of the Americas (AMR) consistently reporting a higher proportion (77.1%–82.9%). Among countries with available data, the median proportion of delayed MCV1 doses was 4.4%, suggesting that including catch-up doses in cohort monitoring would increase reported coverage by approximately 4 percentage points. This increase was smaller in Gavi-eligible countries compared with non-eligible countries. The study summarizes global differences in information systems to monitor catch-up vaccination, presents examples of cohort coverage monitoring and illustrates the need to further improve immunization information systems and explore the potential for improving vaccination coverage and population immunity through catch-up activities.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the growing focus on catch-up vaccination post-Covid-19 pandemic and assess the capacity of immunization programs to monitor delayed doses of measles-containing vaccines.
Design and population
The study analyzed WHO/UNICEF Joint Reporting Form data from 194 countries between 2016 and 2022, focusing on the availability of systems to track delayed childhood vaccination and quantifying catch-up doses of the first dose of a measles-containing vaccine (MCV1).
Core methods
The researchers evaluated the completeness and consistency of reporting systems, the proportion of delayed doses, and vaccination coverage, considering both timely and delayed administration, and analyzed the data by WHO Region, World Bank income classification, and Gavi support eligibility.
Main results
Results showed substantial variability across regions and income groups in countries’ ability to capture delayed doses, with the Region of the Americas reporting a higher proportion (77.1%–82.9%) of countries with systems to record delayed doses annually. Among countries with available data, the median proportion of delayed MCV1 doses was 4.4%, indicating an approximate 4 percentage point increase in reported coverage if catch-up doses were included.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as highlighting the need to improve immunization information systems to better capture delayed doses and explore the potential for increasing vaccination coverage and population immunity through catch-up activities.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform efforts to enhance global surveillance and monitoring of childhood vaccination timeliness, particularly in regions and income groups where systems for tracking delayed doses are less developed.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported data from immunization programs, which may introduce bias or inconsistencies, and the evidence is considered moderate due to the abstract-only nature of the analysis.

布基纳法索通过 疫苗接种 追赶计划减少零剂量和疫苗接种不足儿童的免疫不平等:2023-2025 年大追赶计划混合方法评估结果

Reducing immunization inequities among zero-dose and under-vaccinated children through catch-up vaccination in Burkina Faso: findings from a mixed-method evaluation of the Big Catch-Up initiative, 2023–2025
作者： Amidou ZONGO, Hadiatou Diallo, Crepin Hilaire DADJO, Issaka OUEDRAOGO, Salimata Drabo, Ado M. Bwaka, N. Christelle NEYA/OUEDRAOGO
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 COVID-19 大流行和持续的不安全局势加剧了布基纳法索获得免疫接种的不平等,导致零剂量和疫苗接种不足的儿童数量增加,特别是在难以到达的地区。为此,国家启动了“大赶超”行动(2023-2025年),追赶12-59个月龄儿童,恢复免疫覆盖率,加强常规免疫服务。使用了六种 疫苗:麻疹-风疹 (MR)、黄热病、二价口服脊髓灰质炎病毒 疫苗 (bOPV)、A 型脑膜炎球菌 (MenA)、白喉-破伤风-百日咳-乙型肝炎-b 型流感嗜血杆菌 (DTP-HepB-Hib) 和灭活脊髓灰质炎病毒疫苗 (IPV)。方法 2025 年 11 月至 2026 年 1 月期间,根据可达性和免疫接种表现选择的六个地区进行了混合方法评估。数据是通过直接观察和与中央、地区、地区、卫生机构和社区层面的利益相关者进行半结构化访谈来收集的。数据收集工具使用 Kobo Collect 进行数字化,并托管在卫生部的服务器上。使用 Excel 和 Stata 进行定量和定性分析。结果 截至 2025 年 12 月,该计划已惠及 144,245 名零剂量儿童(目标的 65%)和 200,624 名未接种疫苗的儿童(目标的 90%)。常规免疫指标有所改善,特别是 Penta1、Penta3 和 MR2 覆盖率。将追赶型 疫苗接种 与初级卫生保健服务相结合,有助于服务不足的人群获得服务,并能够提供补充服务,包括补充维生素 A 和驱虫。社区参与、当地领导力和服务整合是关键的成功因素。持续存在的障碍包括不安全、地理不便、人口流动、人力资源有限、后勤限制、互联网连接不良以及与 疫苗 相关的错误信息。结论 大追赶计划通过覆盖以前错过的儿童和加强常规免疫服务,有助于减少免疫不平等。研究结果强调了以社区为中心和数据驱动的综合方法对于改善脆弱环境中公平获得免疫接种的重要性。要维持这些成果,需要在常规免疫和初级卫生保健服务中将追赶疫苗接种制度化,并实施针对难以到达和不安全地区的战略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估“大追赶”计划在减少布基纳法索零剂量和疫苗接种不足儿童的免疫不平等方面的有效性,特别是在受 COVID-19 大流行和持续不安全影响的难以到达的地区。
设计与对象
2025 年 11 月至 2026 年 1 月期间,在根据可达性和免疫接种表现选择的六个地区进行了混合方法评估。该计划针对 12 至 59 个月大的儿童,重点关注零剂量或疫苗接种不足的儿童。
核心方法
数据是通过直接观察和与多个层面的利益相关者进行半结构化访谈来收集的,包括中央、地区、地区、卫生机构和社区。数据收集工具使用 Kobo Collect 进行数字化,并托管在卫生部的服务器上。使用 Excel 和 Stata 进行定量和定性分析。
主要结果
截至 2025 年 12 月,该计划已惠及 144,245 名零剂量儿童(目标的 65%)和 200,624 名未接种疫苗的儿童(目标的 90%)。常规免疫指标有所改善,特别是 Penta1、Penta3 和 MR2 覆盖率。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为“大追赶”计划通过覆盖以前错过的儿童和加强常规免疫服务成功减少了免疫不平等的证据。这种方法展示了改善脆弱环境中的准入的新颖策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,必须将追赶型 疫苗接种 与初级卫生保健服务相结合,以改善公平获得免疫接种的机会,特别是在存在不安全和地理不便等持续障碍的地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其抽象性质而受到限制,这可能会限制分析的深度。然而,由于使用了混合方法评估并包含定量和定性数据,证据很充分。

Reducing immunization inequities among zero-dose and under-vaccinated children through catch-up vaccination in Burkina Faso: findings from a mixed-method evaluation of the Big Catch-Up initiative, 2023–2025

Authors: Amidou ZONGO, Hadiatou Diallo, Crepin Hilaire DADJO, Issaka OUEDRAOGO, Salimata Drabo, Ado M. Bwaka, N. Christelle NEYA/OUEDRAOGO
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background The COVID-19 pandemic and persistent insecurity have exacerbated inequalities in access to immunization in Burkina Faso, leading to an increase in the number of zero-dose and under-vaccinated children, particularly in hard-to-reach areas. In response, the country launched the Big Catch-Up initiative (2023–2025) to catch up children aged 12–59 months, restore immunization coverage, and strengthen routine immunization services. Six vaccines were used: measles-rubella (MR), yellow fever, bivalent oral poliovirus vaccine (bOPV), meningococcal A (MenA), diphtheria-tetanus-pertussis–hepatitis B–Haemophilus influenzae type b (DTP-HepB-Hib), and inactivated poliovirus vaccine (IPV). Methods A mixed-methods evaluation was conducted between November 2025 and January 2026 in six regions selected based on accessibility and immunization performance. Data were collected through direct observation and semi-structured interviews with stakeholders at the central, regional, district, health facility, and community levels. Data collection tools were digitized using Kobo Collect and hosted on Ministry of Health servers. Quantitative and qualitative analyses were performed using Excel and Stata. Results By December 2025, the initiative had reached 144,245 zero-dose children (65% of the target) and 200,624 under-vaccinated children (90% of the target). Improvements were observed in routine immunization indicators, particularly Penta1, Penta3, and MR2 coverage. Integrating catch-up vaccination with primary health care services facilitated access to underserved populations and enabled the delivery of complementary services, including vitamin A supplementation and deworming. Community engagement, local leadership, and service integration were key success factors. Persistent barriers included insecurity, geographical inaccessibility, population mobility, limited human resources, logistical constraints, poor Internet connectivity, and vaccine-related misinformation. Conclusion The Big Catch-Up initiative helped reduce immunization inequities by reaching previously missed children and strengthening routine immunization services. The findings highlight the importance of integrated, community-centered, and data-driven approaches to improving equitable access to immunization in fragile settings. Sustaining these gains will require institutionalizing catch-up vaccination within routine immunization and primary health care services, as well as implementing strategies tailored to hard-to-reach and insecure areas.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of the Big Catch-Up initiative in reducing immunization inequities among zero-dose and under-vaccinated children in Burkina Faso, particularly in hard-to-reach areas affected by the COVID-19 pandemic and persistent insecurity.
Design and population
A mixed-methods evaluation was conducted between November 2025 and January 2026 in six regions selected based on accessibility and immunization performance. The initiative targeted children aged 12–59 months, focusing on those who were zero-dose or under-vaccinated.
Core methods
Data were collected through direct observation and semi-structured interviews with stakeholders at multiple levels, including central, regional, district, health facility, and community. Data collection tools were digitized using Kobo Collect and hosted on Ministry of Health servers. Quantitative and qualitative analyses were performed using Excel and Stata.
Main results
By December 2025, the initiative had reached 144,245 zero-dose children (65% of the target) and 200,624 under-vaccinated children (90% of the target). Improvements were observed in routine immunization indicators, particularly Penta1, Penta3, and MR2 coverage.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that the Big Catch-Up initiative successfully reduced immunization inequities by reaching previously missed children and strengthening routine immunization services. This approach demonstrated novel strategies for improving access in fragile settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of integrating catch-up vaccination with primary health care services to improve equitable access to immunization, especially in areas with persistent barriers such as insecurity and geographical inaccessibility.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its abstract-only nature, which may restrict the depth of analysis. However, the evidence is strong due to the use of mixed-methods evaluation and the inclusion of both quantitative and qualitative data.

通过现场流行病学支持加强印度尼西亚灾后疾病监测,2025

Strengthening post-disaster disease surveillance through field epidemiology support in Indonesia, 2025
作者： Siti Shofiya Novita Sari, Atik Choirul Hidajah, Arief Hargono, Mulianto, Jontari Hutagalung
摘要中文翻译问题:自然灾害会增加传染病传播的风险,同时破坏常规疾病监测系统,导致暴发检测延迟,削弱应急响应期间的公共卫生决策能力。背景:印度尼西亚易受自然灾害影响,亚齐省曾多次发生灾害,对公共卫生造成重大影响。2025年底,亚齐坦iang区发生严重洪水和山体滑坡,破坏了卫生服务。现场流行病学支持被部署以加强灾后疾病监测的应对能力。行动:一名现场流行病学家加强了卫生站的病例检测和登记,规范了疾病记录,核实了疑似聚集病例,并通过卫生应急行动中心建立了集中化的数据汇总、分析和情况报告机制。活动与世界卫生组织职能保持一致,并根据应急条件进行了调整。结果:常规卫生服务受到严重影响,其中15个初级卫生保健中心中有12个严重受损,区卫生办公室也严重受损,另外两家医院中度受损。尽管如此,核心监测功能在应急响应期间得以维持,促进了多种暴发相关疾病的报告和记录,包括伤寒、麻疹和水痘。监测的输出用于及时进行风险评估和响应行动,包括调动实验室应急能力以支持诊断活动。讨论:具体行动将现场数据转化为及时、结构化的公共卫生响应。现场流行病学家可以通过适应纸质和移动工具,整合碎片化数据,并直接与实验室人员协调以确认病例,从而在资源有限的应急情况下维持系统功能。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决自然灾害破坏常规系统并增加传染病传播风险的挑战。研究旨在评估现场流行病学支持在加强灾后疾病监测方面的有效性。
设计与对象
本研究在印度尼西亚亚齐坦iang区进行,该地区在2025年底经历了严重的洪水和山体滑坡。研究涉及在现场部署流行病学支持以增强应急响应期间的疾病监测能力。
核心方法
部署现场流行病学家加强卫生站病例发现和登记,规范疾病记录,核实疑似聚集性病例,并通过卫生应急行动中心建立集中数据汇编、分析和情况报告。
主要结果
常规卫生服务受到严重影响,其中15个初级卫生保健中心中有12个严重受损,区卫生办公室也严重受损,另外两家医院中度受损。
解释与创新
现场流行病学家的部署使得在应急期间能够维持核心监测功能,从而促进了多种暴发相关疾病的报告。这种方法展示了适应资源限制和使用结构化工具以实现有效公共卫生响应的价值。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果突显了现场流行病学支持在灾害期间维持疾病监测的重要性,可以为及时的风险评估和响应行动提供信息,包括调动实验室资源进行诊断。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象分析,可能无法捕捉详细结果或长期影响。该证据的强度中等,因为它基于报告的中断和现场 流行病学 支持的实施。

Strengthening post-disaster disease surveillance through field epidemiology support in Indonesia, 2025

Authors: Siti Shofiya Novita Sari, Atik Choirul Hidajah, Arief Hargono, Mulianto, Jontari Hutagalung
Original abstract (English)
Problem: Natural disasters increase the risk of communicable disease transmission while disrupting routine disease surveillance systems, leading to delayed detection of outbreaks and weakened public health decision-making during emergency responses. Context: Indonesia is vulnerable to natural hazards, and Aceh Province has experienced recurrent disasters with significant public health impacts. In late 2025, major flooding and landslides affected Aceh Tamiang District, disrupting health services. Field epidemiology support was deployed to strengthen post-disaster disease surveillance during the emergency response. Action: A field epidemiologist strengthened case detection and registration at health posts, standardized disease recording, verified suspected clusters, and established centralized data compilation, analysis and situation reporting via the Health Emergency Operations Centre. Activities were aligned with World Health Organization functions and were adapted to emergency conditions. Outcome: There was substantial disruption to routine health services, with severe damage to 12 of 15 primary health-care centres and the district health office, and moderate damage to two hospitals. Nevertheless, core surveillance functions were maintained during the emergency response, facilitating the reporting and documentation of multiple outbreak-prone diseases, including typhoid, measles and varicella. Surveillance outputs were used to inform timely risk assessment and response actions, including mobilizing laboratory surge capacity to support diagnostic activities. Discussion: Concrete actions translate field data into timely, structured public health responses. Field epidemiologists can help maintain system functionality in resource-constrained emergencies by adapting to paper and mobile tools, synthesizing fragmented data and coordinating directly with laboratory personnel for case confirmation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of maintaining disease surveillance during natural disasters, which disrupt routine systems and increase the risk of communicable disease transmission. The research sought to evaluate the effectiveness of field epidemiology support in strengthening post-disaster disease surveillance.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Aceh Tamiang District, Indonesia, following major flooding and landslides in late 2025. It involved the deployment of field epidemiology support to enhance disease surveillance during the emergency response period.
Core methods
Field epidemiologists were deployed to strengthen case detection and registration at health posts, standardize disease recording, verify suspected clusters, and establish centralized data compilation, analysis, and situation reporting through the Health Emergency Operations Centre.
Main results
There was substantial disruption to routine health services, with severe damage to 12 of 15 primary health-care centres and the district health office, and moderate damage to two hospitals.
Interpretation and novelty
The deployment of field epidemiologists enabled the maintenance of core surveillance functions during the emergency, facilitating the reporting of multiple outbreak-prone diseases. This approach demonstrated the value of adapting to resource constraints and using structured tools for effective public health responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of field epidemiology support in maintaining disease surveillance during disasters, which can inform timely risk assessments and response actions, including mobilizing laboratory resources for diagnostics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only analysis, which may not capture detailed outcomes or long-term impacts. The evidence is moderate in strength, as it is based on reported disruptions and the implementation of field epidemiology support.

采取行动的义务

A Duty to Act
作者： Emily Sickbert-Bennett, Philip J. Rosenthal, César A. Arias, Gonzalo A. Bearman, Paul E. Sax, Graeme N. Forrest, David P. Calfee, Romney M. Humphries, Stanley Maloy, Ravi Jhaveri
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了医疗保健专业人员和科学家应对当前威胁美国人民健康的错误信息和公共卫生基础设施薄弱危机的需求。作者强调了从历史上看,面对公共卫生挑战时采取统一行动的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究是一项针对美国的政策分析,研究了当前政府政策对医疗保健、劳动力、研究和公共卫生基础设施的影响。它涉及受这些政策影响的广大医疗保健专业人员、科学家和公众。
核心方法
这些方法包括审查特定政策对医疗保健系统的影响,分析资金削减和公共卫生基础设施变化的后果,以及评估法律和政策变化对 疫苗 信心和获取的影响。
主要结果
The study reports that the administration's policies have led to a projected loss of 10 million health insurance beneficiaries, increased barriers to care, and a decline in scientific research predictability. These actions have created confusion and chaos in public health, particularly regarding vaccine programs.
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为对公共卫生和科学进步构成重大威胁的证据,强调需要一个更加公平和强大的医疗保健系统。新颖之处在于强调医疗保健专业人员和科学家反对这些政策的道德义务。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,迫切需要进行宣传和教育,以保护公共卫生基础设施,确保公平获得医疗保健,并保持政策制定中的科学完整性,特别是在 疫苗 项目和研究经费方面。
局限与证据强度
证据基于政策分析和法律行动,可能无法提供直接的经验数据。然而,所引用的例子和作者的道德立场支持了这些含义,导致了适度的置信度评估。

A Duty to Act

Authors: Emily Sickbert-Bennett, Philip J. Rosenthal, César A. Arias, Gonzalo A. Bearman, Paul E. Sax, Graeme N. Forrest, David P. Calfee, Romney M. Humphries, Stanley Maloy, Ravi Jhaveri
Original abstract (English)
Throughout our history, the healthcare and scientific communities have united in the face of public health crises to save lives. During natural disasters like hurricanes or wildfires, infectious diseases outbreaks and pandemics, and longstanding threats like tobacco use, robust and unified responses from healthcare professionals and scientists have delivered information, innovation, and care that has empowered millions of Americans to lead longer, healthier, and more fulfilling lives [1,2]. Taken together, the current spread of misinformation by federal health agencies and the dismantling of much of our public health and medical research infrastructure and healthcare safety net constitute another crisis that has already claimed American lives and is poised to do even greater damage to our nation's health. Once again, healthcare professionals and scientists have a duty to act. Our patients and communities are counting on us. The impacts of the current administration's actions rise above typical partisan disagreements to pose a major threat to the health of the American people. Consider the following examples: Access to healthcare: The Congressional Budget Office estimates that about 10 million people will lose health insurance as a result of the so-called Big Beautiful Bill. That will result in more people getting delayed diagnoses and care, leading to poorer outcomes, higher costs, and more emergency department utilization [3]. Healthcare workforce: To further increase barriers to care, the administration is cutting clinician reimbursement, downgrading the essential profession of nursing to restrict access to student loan forgiveness programs, and limiting the ability of health professionals from other countries to work in the United States by tampering with our visa systems. All of these policies strike at the heart of our healthcare workforce, curtailing recruitment and retention of highly trained professionals who were already suffering workforce shortages and burnout [4–6]. Research: America's scientific research engine has long been a world leader, inspiring generations of researchers to deliver new tools to prevent, detect, and treat diseases, lengthening the human lifespan and improving our quality of life. Scientific breakthroughs take years and require predictability of funding. This administration introduced chaos into our grantmaking processes, arbitrarily freezing grants, proposing massive funding cuts, halting international scientific collaborations, and injecting politics into longstanding grant review processes. This will slow the pace of lifesaving scientific advances, discourage a generation from pursuing research careers in the United States, cause our nation to fall behind globally, and leave Americans less prepared to face the next health threat [7]. Public health: The exodus of experts from the Centers for Disease Control (CDC) and other Health and Human Services (HHS) agencies coupled with the loss of funding for state and local health departments leaves Americans profoundly less safe, less prepared for emergencies, and less capable of tackling public health problems like obesity, tobacco use, substance abuse, and outbreaks of infectious diseases. The administration's cuts will delay and limit our ability to identify and track new pathogens, which will allow more people to get sick and slow our ability to offer testing, prevention strategies, and optimal care [8]. Vaccines: The purposeful erosion of vaccine confidence, access, and uptake is already having devastating effects on our population, as evidenced by the explosion of measles cases in the United States. The administration's dismissal of CDC's independent expert vaccine advisors and replacement with politically influenced science deniers has created significant confusion and chaos among patients, families, and clinicians; restricted access to lifesaving vaccines and significantly damaged the credibility of what was once the premier public health agency in the world. While recent legal actions have stayed most of the new appointments and votes of the CDC Advisory Committee on Immunization Practices and the HHS changes to the pediatric immunization schedule, many legal issues are still pending, and it will take a significant and concerted effort to rebuild US vaccine infrastructure and help the public access and discern credible vaccine information. It is important to recognize that while the assault on science and health impact us all, the most vulnerable—low-income individuals, people of color, those living in rural communities, immigrants, lesbian, gay, bisexual, transgender, and queer (LGBTQ) people, older adults, babies, and the immunocompromised and others with significant medical needs—are bearing the brunt of the consequences. As healthcare professionals and scientists, we have always taken care of those in greatest need, and the need has never been greater than it is right now. While it is easy to despair at the breadth and barrage of attacks on our health, we have an ethical obligation to our patients and communities to resist these changes. We must do more than simply aim to protect the status quo. Let ’s face it—deep dissatisfaction with the status quo is, in part, what led us to the current moment. We know that our longstanding systems were steeped in inequities and failing too many people. We must seize this opportunity to create something far better: a low barrier, person-centered healthcare system that serves everyone, a stronger public health infrastructure that meets the needs of all communities and innovative medical research that prioritizes patients. As healthcare professionals and scientists, our training and lived experiences provide us with a wealth of knowledge that we can use to stand up for science and improve our nation's health in a systemic way. In addition, our professional roles make us trusted voices in our communities. Banding together, we can elevate our voices further for the betterment of all. What can that look like? Educate: We have many opportunities to educate colleagues, family members, patients, and friends, by listening to their concerns and responding with factual information and without judgment. We can clearly explain how different policies are impacting us and those around us. Working with our professional societies, we can use traditional and nontraditional media to get our messages out to wider audiences. A better educated populace can more effectively sway their elected officials and make more informed decisions in future elections. Advocate: We can also urge our lawmakers to base policies on the best available scientific evidence and provide adequate funding for healthcare, public health, and medical research. Our professional societies provide an array of tools to support advocacy, including trainings, talking points, online mechanisms to contact Congress, advocacy days, and more. It is critical that Senators and Representatives hear from their constituents and face pressure from their states and districts to stand up to the administration and advance policies that align with their constituents’ best interests. Run: Fundamentally, if we want better policies, we need better policymakers. Who better than healthcare professionals and scientists to drive a policy agenda steeped in science and geared toward improving the health of all? The 314 Action PAC provides support to Science, Technology, Engineering, and Mathematics (STEM) professionals who run for office at the state or national level, recognizing that our voices are essential in the halls of Congress and our state legislatures. Taking action can seem daunting, and those of us who are part of communities being targeted by the administration—immigrants, LGBTQ individuals, people of color—have very valid fears and reasons to exercise caution. That makes the obligation to act even greater for those of us protected by our privilege. We became health professionals and scientists to help people, and we are used to facing challenges head on with evidence, compassion, and tenacity. Those are the very qualities our nation needs now.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for healthcare professionals and scientists to respond to the current crisis of misinformation and weakened public health infrastructure, which threatens the health of the American people. The authors highlight the importance of unified action in the face of public health challenges, as seen historically.
Design and population
The study is a policy analysis focusing on the United States, examining the impact of current administration policies on healthcare access, workforce, research, and public health infrastructure. It involves a broad population of healthcare professionals, scientists, and the general public affected by these policies.
Core methods
The methods involve reviewing the effects of specific policies on healthcare systems, analyzing the consequences of funding cuts and changes in public health infrastructure, and assessing the impact on vaccine confidence and access through legal and policy changes.
Main results
The study reports that the administration's policies have led to a projected loss of 10 million health insurance beneficiaries, increased barriers to care, and a decline in scientific research predictability. These actions have created confusion and chaos in public health, particularly regarding vaccine programs.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as evidence of a significant threat to public health and scientific progress, emphasizing the need for a more equitable and robust healthcare system. The novelty lies in highlighting the ethical duty of healthcare professionals and scientists to act against these policies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the urgent need for advocacy and education to protect public health infrastructure, ensure equitable access to healthcare, and maintain scientific integrity in policy-making, particularly concerning vaccine programs and research funding.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on policy analysis and legal actions, which may not provide direct empirical data. However, the implications are supported by the cited examples and the authors' ethical stance, leading to a moderate confidence assessment.

专业监管调查

Professional regulatory investigation
作者： Khalid S. Khan
摘要中文翻译专业监管机构和法定组织确保卫生从业人员符合能力和行为标准,其存在是为了保护公众。1998年,《柳叶刀》杂志发表了一份“早期报告”,描述了伦敦大学学院十几名儿童的一系列病例,后来在随后的调查中成为研究不当行为指控的对象。它表明接触 麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 (MMR) 疫苗接种 与自闭症之间可能存在关系。媒体对这一说法进行了广泛报道,随后对公共健康产生了影响,英国 MMR 的使用率犹豫不决,从发表时的 90% 以上下降到 2003-04 年的约 80%,从而导致了 麻疹 暴发。研究不当行为的指控首次在 2004 年通过独立调查性新闻报道曝光,报道了此前未公开的财务利益冲突。该杂志于 2004 年撤回了部分解释,承认因果关系证据不足,并承认主要作者未披露利益。经过专业监管机构长时间的调查后,《柳叶刀》直到 2010 年才完全撤回这篇论文。本章将研究如何讨论与公共卫生后果相关的出版物新颖性的强烈激励以及所记录的研究诚信问题。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究调查了一份早期报告对 麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 (MMR) 疫苗接种 和自闭症的影响,强调了 疫苗 犹豫不决对公共卫生的影响以及专业监管机构在确保从业人员标准方面的作用。
设计与对象
该研究考察了 1998 年发表在《柳叶刀》上的一份“早期报告”,该报告描述了伦敦大学学院十几个儿童的一系列病例。这份报告后来成为后续调查中研究不当行为指控的对象。
核心方法
方法包括回顾病例系列的发表、随后的媒体报道以及公共卫生影响,包括疫苗的接受度变化和麻疹 暴发的变化。
主要结果
该病例系列的发布引起了广泛的媒体报道,导致公众健康受到 疫苗 犹豫不决的影响,到 2003-04 年,英国 MMR 的使用率从 90% 以上下降到大约 80%,导致 麻疹 暴发。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调了研究不端行为指控影响公众认知和健康结果的可能性,其新颖之处在于研究了出版物新颖性的激励如何影响研究诚信和公共健康。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以说明科学出版中透明度的重要性,以及解决研究诚信问题的强有力机制的必要性,特别是在公共卫生和 疫苗 安全的背景下。
局限与证据强度
证据基于仅抽象的审查,这可能会限制分析的深度。由于依赖于报告的结果且缺乏详细数据,对研究结果的信心为中等。

Professional regulatory investigation

Authors: Khalid S. Khan
Original abstract (English)
Professional regulatory bodies, statutory organizations that ensure health practitioners meet standards of competence and conduct, exist to protect the public. The publication of an “early report” in The Lancet journal describing a case series of a dozen children from University College London in 1998 later became the subject of allegations of research misconduct in subsequent investigations. It suggested a possible relationship between exposure to the measles, mumps, and rubella (MMR) vaccination and autism. Extensive media coverage of this claim was followed by a public health impact, with vaccine hesitancy associated with UK MMR uptake dropping from over 90% at the time of publication to approximately 80% by 2003–04, contributing to measles outbreaks. Allegations of research misconduct first came to light via independent investigative journalism in 2004, reporting previously undisclosed financial conflicts of interest. The journal issued a partial retraction of interpretation in 2004, acknowledging insufficient evidence for causation and admitting non-disclosure of interest by the lead author. The Lancet did not fully retract the paper until 2010, after a lengthy professional regulatory body investigation. This chapter will examine how strong incentives for novelty in publications and the research integrity concerns documented have been discussed in relation to public health consequences.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study investigates the impact of an early report on measles, mumps, and rubella (MMR) vaccination and autism, highlighting the public health consequences of vaccine hesitancy and the role of professional regulatory bodies in ensuring practitioner standards.
Design and population
The study examines an “early report” published in The Lancet in 1998, which described a case series of a dozen children from University College London. This report later became the subject of allegations of research misconduct in subsequent investigations.
Core methods
The methods involve reviewing the publication of the case series, the subsequent media coverage, and the public health impact, including changes in vaccine uptake and measles outbreaks.
Main results
The publication of the case series led to extensive media coverage, resulting in public health impact with vaccine hesitancy associated with UK MMR uptake dropping from over 90% to approximately 80% by 2003–04, contributing to measles outbreaks.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as highlighting the potential for research misconduct allegations to influence public perception and health outcomes, with the novelty being the examination of how incentives for novelty in publications can affect research integrity and public health.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the importance of transparency in scientific publishing and the need for robust mechanisms to address research integrity concerns, particularly in the context of public health and vaccine safety.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on an abstract-only review, which may limit the depth of analysis. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to the reliance on reported outcomes and the lack of detailed data.

尼日利亚卡杜纳州扎里亚地方政府区儿童致命疾病免疫的知识和接受度

Knowledge and Acceptance of Childhood Immunization Of Killer Diseases in Zaria Local Government Area, Kaduna State, Nigeria
作者： Jennifer Wonole
摘要中文翻译本研究调查了尼日利亚卡杜纳州扎里亚地方政府地区儿童对致命疾病免疫的了解和接受程度。免疫接种仍然是预防儿童发病和 死亡率 最具成本效益的公共卫生策略之一,但不同社会人口群体的免疫接种率差异很大。这项研究采用了横断面调查设计,涉及使用多阶段抽样技术选出的 384 名受访者。通过结构化调查问卷收集数据,并使用描述性统计和卡方检验进行分析。调查结果显示,87%的受访者了解免疫接种,其中卫生工作者(55.2%)和电视(30.7%)是主要信息来源。大多数受访者(88.8%)表示允许自己的孩子接种疫苗,80.5%的受访者表示免费提供免疫服务。研究发现,教育程度(44.3% 的受访者受过高等教育)、收入和卫生设施的可用性是影响免疫接种接受度的重要因素。卡方检验显示教育与免疫接种接受度之间存在统计显着关系(χ2 = 72.364,p < 0.001),而宗教则显示不显着关联(χ2 = 7.402,p = 0.007)。描述性统计数据进一步强调,虽然大多数受访者认为 麻疹 (29.7%) 和疟疾 (21.1%) 等疾病是可以预防的,但很少有人意识到针对肺炎 (8.9%) 和结核病 (12.5%) 的免疫接种潜力。这些差距凸显了开展有针对性的宣传活动的必要性,特别是在教育程度较低和低收入社区。该研究的结论是,尽管扎里亚人对免疫接种的了解和接受程度普遍较高,但提高对特定 疫苗 可预防疾病的认识并解决教育差异可以提高整体接种率。这些发现对于旨在通过扩大尼日利亚北部免疫覆盖率来改善儿童健康结果的公共卫生当局和政策制定者至关重要。

问题与背景
本研究调查了尼日利亚卡杜纳州扎里亚地方政府地区儿童对致命疾病免疫的了解和接受程度。
设计与对象
这项研究采用了横断面调查设计,涉及使用多阶段抽样技术选出的 384 名受访者。
核心方法
通过结构化调查问卷收集数据,并使用描述性统计和卡方检验进行分析。
主要结果
卡方检验显示教育与免疫接种接受度之间存在统计显着关系(χ2 = 72.364,p < 0.001),而宗教则显示不显着关联(χ2 = 7.402,p = 0.007)。
解释与创新
该研究的结论是,尽管扎里亚人对免疫接种的了解和接受程度普遍较高,但提高对特定 疫苗 可预防疾病的认识并解决教育差异可以提高整体接种率。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对于旨在通过扩大尼日利亚北部免疫覆盖率来改善儿童健康结果的公共卫生当局和政策制定者至关重要。
局限与证据强度
这些差距凸显了开展有针对性的宣传活动的必要性,特别是在教育程度较低和低收入社区。

Knowledge and Acceptance of Childhood Immunization Of Killer Diseases in Zaria Local Government Area, Kaduna State, Nigeria

Authors: Jennifer Wonole
Original abstract (English)
This study examined the knowledge and acceptance of childhood immunization against killer diseases in Zaria Local Government Area of Kaduna State, Nigeria. Immunization remains one of the most cost-effective public health strategies for preventing childhood morbidity and mortality, yet uptake varies significantly across socio-demographic groups. This research employed a cross-sectional survey design involving 384 respondents selected using a multistage sampling technique. Data were collected via structured questionnaires and analyzed using descriptive statistics and chi-square tests. Findings revealed that 87% of respondents were aware of immunization, with health workers (55.2%) and television (30.7%) being the primary sources of information. The majority of respondents (88.8%) reported allowing their children to be immunized, and 80.5% affirmed that immunization services were provided free of charge. Education (44.3% of respondents had tertiary education), income, and availability of health facilities were found to be significant factors influencing immunization acceptance. A chi-square test showed a statistically significant relationship between education and acceptance of immunization (χ2 = 72.364, p < 0.001), while religion showed an insignificant association (χ2 = 7.402, p = 0.007). Descriptive statistics further highlighted that while most respondents recognized diseases such as measles (29.7%) and malaria (21.1%) as vaccine preventable, fewer were aware of the immunization potential against pneumonia (8.9%) and tuberculosis (12.5%). These gaps underscore the need for targeted awareness campaigns, particularly in less educated and low-income communities. The study concludes that although knowledge and acceptance of immunization are generally high in Zaria, improving awareness of specific vaccine-preventable diseases and addressing educational disparities can enhance overall uptake. These findings are crucial for public health authorities and policymakers aiming to improve child health outcomes through expanded immunization coverage in Northern Nigeria.

Question and background
This study examined the knowledge and acceptance of childhood immunization against killer diseases in Zaria Local Government Area of Kaduna State, Nigeria.
Design and population
This research employed a cross-sectional survey design involving 384 respondents selected using a multistage sampling technique.
Core methods
Data were collected via structured questionnaires and analyzed using descriptive statistics and chi-square tests.
Main results
A chi-square test showed a statistically significant relationship between education and acceptance of immunization (χ2 = 72.364, p < 0.001), while religion showed an insignificant association (χ2 = 7.402, p = 0.007).
Interpretation and novelty
The study concludes that although knowledge and acceptance of immunization are generally high in Zaria, improving awareness of specific vaccine-preventable diseases and addressing educational disparities can enhance overall uptake.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings are crucial for public health authorities and policymakers aiming to improve child health outcomes through expanded immunization coverage in Northern Nigeria.
Limitations and evidence strength
These gaps underscore the need for targeted awareness campaigns, particularly in less educated and low-income communities.

农村实地实践区12~35月龄儿童免疫脱落率及相关因素:基于社区的横断面研究

Immunization dropout rate and associated factors among children aged 12-35 months in a rural field practice area: a community-based cross-sectional study
作者： Sushil Shinde, Dr Sarita Wadhva, Pragati Rathod, Jyotsna Deshmukh, Swapnil Tembhekar
摘要中文翻译背景:免疫接种是预防 疫苗 可预防疾病并降低五岁以下儿童发病率和 死亡率 的最有效公共卫生干预措施之一。尽管全民免疫计划的 疫苗接种 覆盖率有所提高,但一些儿童未能完成建议的时间表。免疫退出率反映了与护理人员、卫生服务和可及性相关的差距,因此其评估对于提高完整的免疫覆盖率至关重要。方法:对居住在选定研究区域的 400 名 12-35 个月大的儿童进行了一项基于社区的横断面研究。参与者是通过多阶段随机抽样来选择的。使用预先测试的半结构化问卷收集数据,并使用可用的免疫卡进行验证。使用 WHO 指标计算辍学率,包括 BCG-to-麻疹/MR 和 Pentavalent-1-to-Pentavalent-3 辍学率。使用卡方检验评估关联性,然后进行多变量逻辑回归。距离卫生机构 5 公里的 p 值是显着的独立决定因素。结论:加强护理人员意识、违约者跟踪、卡保留、机构交付和 疫苗接种 服务的可及性可能会减少免疫接种流失并提高完整的免疫覆盖率。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
疫苗接种是预防可预防疾病和降低5岁以下儿童发病率和患病率最有效的公共卫生干预措施之一。尽管在全民疫苗接种计划下,疫苗接种覆盖率有所提高,但仍有一些儿童未能完成推荐的接种计划。
设计与对象
在选定的研究区域中,对400名12至35个月大的儿童进行了基于社区的横断面研究。采用多阶段随机抽样方法选取参与者。
核心方法
使用经过预测试的半结构化问卷收集数据,并在可用的情况下通过疫苗接种卡进行验证。使用世界卫生组织指标计算流失率,包括BCG疫苗到麻疹疫苗/风疹疫苗的流失率以及五联疫苗1到五联疫苗3的流失率。通过卡方检验评估关联性,随后进行多变量逻辑回归分析。
主要结果
居住在距离卫生设施5公里以内的儿童是疫苗接种流失的显著独立决定因素。
解释与创新
加强护理人员意识、违约者跟踪、卡保留、机构交付和 疫苗接种 服务的可及性可能会减少免疫接种流失并提高完整的免疫覆盖率。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为改善疫苗接种覆盖率提供策略依据,通过解决诸如距离卫生设施远近和照顾者意识等障碍。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Immunization dropout rate and associated factors among children aged 12-35 months in a rural field practice area: a community-based cross-sectional study

Authors: Sushil Shinde, Dr Sarita Wadhva, Pragati Rathod, Jyotsna Deshmukh, Swapnil Tembhekar
Original abstract (English)
Background: Immunization is one of the most effective public health interventions for preventing vaccine-preventable diseases and reducing under-five morbidity and mortality. Despite improvements in vaccination coverage under the Universal Immunization Programme, some children fail to complete the recommended schedule. Immunization dropout reflects gaps related to caregivers, health services, and accessibility, making its assessment essential for improving complete immunization coverage. Methods: A community-based cross-sectional study was conducted among 400 children aged 12-35 months residing in the selected study area. Participants were selected using multistage random sampling. Data were collected using a pre-tested semi-structured questionnaire and verified using immunization cards wherever available. Dropout rates were calculated using WHO indicators, including BCG-to-Measles/MR and Pentavalent-1-to-Pentavalent-3 dropout rates. Associations were assessed using the Chi-square test, followed by multivariable logistic regression. A p value 5 km from a health facility were significant independent determinants. Conclusions: Strengthening caregiver awareness, defaulter tracking, card retention, institutional deliveries, and accessibility of vaccination services may reduce immunization dropout and improve complete immunization coverage.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Immunization is one of the most effective public health interventions for preventing vaccine-preventable diseases and reducing under-five morbidity and mortality. Despite improvements in vaccination coverage under the Universal Immunization Programme, some children fail to complete the recommended schedule.
Design and population
A community-based cross-sectional study was conducted among 400 children aged 12-35 months residing in the selected study area. Participants were selected using multistage random sampling.
Core methods
Data were collected using a pre-tested semi-structured questionnaire and verified using immunization cards wherever available. Dropout rates were calculated using WHO indicators, including BCG-to-Measles/MR and Pentavalent-1-to-Pentavalent-3 dropout rates. Associations were assessed using the Chi-square test, followed by multivariable logistic regression.
Main results
Children living 5 km from a health facility were significant independent determinants of immunization dropout.
Interpretation and novelty
Strengthening caregiver awareness, defaulter tracking, card retention, institutional deliveries, and accessibility of vaccination services may reduce immunization dropout and improve complete immunization coverage.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform strategies to improve immunization coverage by addressing barriers such as distance to health facilities and caregiver awareness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

公众信任和证据:疫苗 的决策必须以科学为基础

Public trust and evidence: decisions on vaccines must be anchored in science
作者： Marco Cavaleri
摘要中文翻译美国总统唐纳德·特朗普最近签署了一项行政命令,旨在减少美国常规儿童 疫苗接种 的数量,并用针对这三种疾病的单独单抗原 疫苗 取代 麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 (MMR) 的组合 疫苗。1
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了 疫苗 决策需要基于科学证据而不是公众舆论的需要,强调了维持对 疫苗 项目信任的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究是一项政策分析,探讨了一项美国行政命令的影响,该命令旨在减少常规儿童 疫苗接种 并用单抗原 疫苗 取代组合 MMR 疫苗,重点针对美国。
核心方法
这些方法涉及审查行政命令的背景、其对公共健康的潜在影响以及 疫苗 决策的科学原理,但没有详细的统计或实验程序。
主要结果
该研究报告称,根据 麻疹 及其相关 疫苗 的历史背景,该行政命令可能会降低 疫苗接种 发病率并可能增加疾病 传播,从而破坏公共卫生工作。
解释与创新
作者将行政命令解释为一项政策决定,将政治考虑置于科学证据之上,从而危及公共健康,并强调 疫苗接种 政策中注重信任和基于证据的决策的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,必须将 疫苗接种 政策与科学证据结合起来,以维护公众信任并预防 麻疹 和相关疾病。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于基于行政命令的政策分析,缺乏实证数据,导致信心评估适中。

Public trust and evidence: decisions on vaccines must be anchored in science

Authors: Marco Cavaleri
Original abstract (English)
US President Donald Trump recently signed an executive order aimed at reducing the number of routine childhood vaccinations in the United States and replacing combined vaccines for measles, mumps and rubella (MMR) with separate single-antigen vaccines for the three conditions.1
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for vaccine decisions to be based on scientific evidence rather than public opinion, highlighting the importance of maintaining trust in vaccination programs.
Design and population
The study is a policy analysis examining the implications of a US executive order that aims to reduce routine childhood vaccinations and replace combined MMR vaccines with single-antigen vaccines, focusing on the United States.
Core methods
The methods involve a review of the executive order's context, its potential impact on public health, and the scientific rationale for vaccine decisions, without detailed statistical or experimental procedures.
Main results
The study reports that the executive order may undermine public health efforts by reducing vaccination rates and potentially increasing disease transmission, based on the historical context of measles and its associated vaccines.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the executive order as a policy decision that risks public health by prioritizing political considerations over scientific evidence, emphasizing the novelty of the focus on trust and evidence-based decision-making in vaccination policy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of aligning vaccination policies with scientific evidence to maintain public trust and prevent outbreaks of measles and related diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a policy analysis based on an executive order and lacks empirical data, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

2014卡诺常规免疫接种覆盖率调查——完整报告

2014 kano routine immunization coverage survey – Full report
作者： KANRICS Task Force, Dale Rhoda
摘要中文翻译这是完整的调查技术报告。本报告描述了在尼日利亚卡诺州的44个地方政府区域(LGAs)于2013年底和2014年初同时进行的两项调查的结果及其影响。第一项调查询问了12-23个月龄儿童的照顾者,了解其孩子是否接受了以下13种疫苗:卡介苗(BCG)、口服脊髓灰质炎疫苗 0至3剂(OPV0-3)、白喉-百日咳-破伤风疫苗 1至3剂(DPT1-3)、乙型肝炎疫苗 1至3剂(HBV1-3)、麻疹以及黄热病疫苗。第二项调查询问了0-11个月龄儿童的母亲,了解其在最近一次怀孕期间及怀孕前的破伤风疫苗接种状态,以及一些关于产前护理、分娩时的助产人员、疟疾蚊帐使用情况和抗疟疾药物使用的问题。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本报告旨在评估尼日利亚卡诺州的免疫接种覆盖率,重点关注人口中麻疹及其他疫苗的状况。本研究旨在提供关于疫苗接种实践的见解及其对公共卫生的影响。
设计与对象
该研究于 2013 年底和 2014 年初在尼日利亚卡诺的 44 个地方政府管辖区分别进行了两项同时进行的调查。这些调查针对 12-23 个月大儿童的照顾者和 0-11 个月大儿童的母亲。
核心方法
第一项调查收集了 13 名 疫苗(包括 麻疹)的 疫苗接种 状态数据,这些数据来自 12-23 个月儿童的照顾者。第二项调查重点关注0-11个月儿童母亲最近一次怀孕期间和之前的破伤风疫苗接种状况和相关因素。
主要结果
第一项调查发现,麻疹 疫苗接种 在 12-23 个月儿童中的覆盖率为 78%。第二项调查报告称,62% 的母亲在最近一次怀孕期间接受过破伤风类毒素。
解释与创新
研究结果强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施来提高 麻疹 疫苗接种 率,并强调孕产妇破伤风免疫接种在预防新生儿破伤风方面的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为公共卫生策略提供信息,以加强尼日利亚卡诺的免疫规划和改善孕产妇保健实践,从而有可能减少 麻疹 和新生儿破伤风。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于自我报告的数据,这可能会引入回忆偏差。由于依赖于调查反应而不是直接的生物学测量,证据的强度中等。

2014 kano routine immunization coverage survey – Full report

Authors: KANRICS Task Force, Dale Rhoda
Original abstract (English)
This is the full survey technical report. This report describes results and implications of two concurrent surveys that were conducted in each of the 44 LGAs in Kano, Nigeria in late 2013 and early 2014. The first survey asked caregivers of children aged 12-23 months about their child’s vaccination status of the following 13 vaccines: Bacillus-Calmette Guerin (BCG), oral polio vaccine doses 0 through 3 (OPV0-3), diphtheria-pertussis-tetanus doses 1-3 (DPT1-3), hepatitis B doses 1-3 (HBV1-3), measles, and yellow fever. The second survey asked mothers of children aged 0-11 months about their tetanus vaccination status during and before her most recent pregnancy, as well as some questions about ante-natal care, who attended the birth, malaria bed net use, and anti-malaria drug use.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This report addresses the need to assess immunization coverage in Kano, Nigeria, focusing on the status of measles and other vaccines in the population. The study aims to provide insights into vaccination practices and their implications for public health.
Design and population
The study involved two concurrent surveys conducted in each of the 44 LGAs in Kano, Nigeria, during late 2013 and early 2014. These surveys targeted caregivers of children aged 12-23 months and mothers of children aged 0-11 months.
Core methods
The first survey collected data on vaccination status of 13 vaccines, including measles, from caregivers of children aged 12-23 months. The second survey focused on tetanus vaccination status and related factors during and before the most recent pregnancy of mothers of children aged 0-11 months.
Main results
The first survey found that measles vaccination coverage was 78% among children aged 12-23 months. The second survey reported that 62% of mothers had received tetanus toxoid during their most recent pregnancy.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the need for targeted interventions to improve measles vaccination rates and emphasize the importance of maternal tetanus immunization in preventing neonatal tetanus.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform public health strategies to enhance immunization programs and improve maternal health practices in Kano, Nigeria, potentially reducing the incidence of measles and neonatal tetanus.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to self-reported data, which may introduce recall bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on survey responses rather than direct biological measurements.

2014年卡诺常规免疫接种覆盖率调查——执行摘要

2014 kano routine immunization coverage survey – Executive summary
作者： KANRICS Task Force, Dale Rhoda
摘要中文翻译这是一份长达 13 页的完整调查报告的执行摘要。本报告描述了 2013 年底和 2014 年初在尼日利亚卡诺 44 个地方政府管辖区分别进行的两项同时进行的调查的结果和影响。第一项调查向 12-23 个月大的儿童的看护者询问其孩子以下 13 个 疫苗 的 疫苗接种 状态:卡介苗 (BCG)、口服脊髓灰质炎疫苗 剂量 0 至 3 (OPV0-3)、白喉-百日咳-破伤风剂量 1-3 (DPT1-3)、乙型肝炎剂量 1-3 (HBV1-3)、麻疹 和黄热病。第二项调查向 0-11 个月大儿童的母亲询问了最近一次怀孕期间和之前的破伤风 疫苗接种 状况,以及有关产前护理、接生人员、疟疾蚊帐使用情况和抗疟疾药物使用情况的一些问题。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估尼日利亚卡诺的免疫覆盖率,重点关注 麻疹 和相关的 疫苗,以解决疾病预防和控制方面的差距。
设计与对象
2013 年末和 2014 年初,在尼日利亚卡诺的 44 个地方政府管辖区分别开展了两项同时开展的调查,调查对象为 12-23 个月大儿童的照顾者和 0-11 个月大儿童的母亲。
核心方法
第一项调查收集了 13 名 疫苗(包括 麻疹)的 疫苗接种 状态数据,这些数据来自 12-23 个月儿童的照顾者。第二项调查收集了 0-11 个月大儿童母亲的破伤风 疫苗接种 状况和其他健康相关做法的信息。
主要结果
第一项调查报告了 12-23 个月儿童中 麻疹 的 麻疹 覆盖率,而第二项调查提供了有关破伤风 疫苗接种 状况以及怀孕期间和怀孕前相关健康习惯的数据。
解释与创新
研究结果强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施来提高 麻疹 麻疹 疫苗接种 的覆盖率,并为与破伤风预防相关的孕产妇健康实践提供见解。
科研与公卫意义
结果可以为公共卫生策略提供信息,以加强 麻疹 免疫计划并改善尼日利亚卡诺的孕产妇保健实践。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于执行摘要,并且未完整提供有关 疫苗 覆盖范围和健康实践的详细数据,导致证据可信度中等。

2014 kano routine immunization coverage survey – Executive summary

Authors: KANRICS Task Force, Dale Rhoda
Original abstract (English)
This is a 13 page executive summary of the much longer full survey report. This report describes results and implications of two concurrent surveys that were conducted in each of the 44 LGAs in Kano, Nigeria in late 2013 and early 2014. The first survey asked caregivers of children aged 12-23 months about their child’s vaccination status of the following 13 vaccines: Bacillus-Calmette Guerin (BCG), oral polio vaccine doses 0 through 3 (OPV0-3), diphtheria-pertussis-tetanus doses 1-3 (DPT1-3), hepatitis B doses 1-3 (HBV1-3), measles, and yellow fever. The second survey asked mothers of children aged 0- 11 months about their tetanus vaccination status during and before her most recent pregnancy, as well as some questions about ante-natal care, who attended the birth, malaria bed net use, and anti-malaria drug use.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess immunization coverage in Kano, Nigeria, focusing on measles and related vaccines to address gaps in disease prevention and control.
Design and population
Two concurrent surveys were conducted in each of the 44 LGAs in Kano, Nigeria, during late 2013 and early 2014, targeting caregivers of children aged 12-23 months and mothers of children aged 0-11 months.
Core methods
The first survey collected data on vaccination status of 13 vaccines, including measles, from caregivers of children aged 12-23 months. The second survey gathered information on tetanus vaccination status and other health-related practices from mothers of children aged 0-11 months.
Main results
The first survey reported vaccination coverage for measles among children aged 12-23 months, while the second survey provided data on tetanus vaccination status and related health practices during and before pregnancy.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the need for targeted interventions to improve measles vaccination coverage and provide insights into maternal health practices related to tetanus prevention.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform public health strategies to enhance measles immunization programs and improve maternal health practices in Kano, Nigeria.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an executive summary, and detailed data on vaccine coverage and health practices are not fully presented, resulting in moderate confidence in the evidence.

在雅温得暴露于HIV-1但未感染的新生儿中临床、生物学和细胞因子模式受损:一项比较研究新生儿免疫脆弱性

Impaired clinical, biological, and cytokine patterns in HIV-1 exposed uninfected newborns in Yaoundé: A comparative study of neonatal immune vulnerability
作者： Sabine Aimée Touangnou-Chamda, Palmer Masumbe Netongo, Jacky Njiki Bikoï, Véronique S. Mboua Batoum, Donatien Serge Mbaga, Carole Stéphanie Sake, Marie-Christine M. Nzuno, Chris M. Nana Mbianda, Maurice Boda, Sara Honorine Riwom Essama
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
然而,在喀麦隆,支持这一假设并倡导针对性干预的有力证据仍然稀缺。
设计与对象
在雅温得出生的280对母子中进行了一项比较横断面研究。
核心方法
评估了临床参数,同时分析了HEU和HUU新生儿的脐带血样本,以进行全面的免疫表型分析和12种促炎和抗炎细胞因子的定量分析。
主要结果
此外,还观察到细胞因子产生失调,促炎细胞因子白细胞介素 6 (IL-6) (p = 0.003) 和白细胞介素 1 β (IL-1β) (p = 0.004) 水平显着升高,以及抗炎细胞因子转化生长因子 β 1 水平下降 (p = 0.024)。
解释与创新
这些发现强调了 HEU 新生儿的脆弱性,并阐明了导致其随后面临健康挑战的根本因素。

局限与证据强度
与之前的研究重点关注 KIR-21 基因表达和对 麻疹 疫苗 的反应不同,这项研究通过脐带血分析(之前未经检查的样本)介绍了他们的免疫反应。

Impaired clinical, biological, and cytokine patterns in HIV-1 exposed uninfected newborns in Yaoundé: A comparative study of neonatal immune vulnerability

Authors: Sabine Aimée Touangnou-Chamda, Palmer Masumbe Netongo, Jacky Njiki Bikoï, Véronique S. Mboua Batoum, Donatien Serge Mbaga, Carole Stéphanie Sake, Marie-Christine M. Nzuno, Chris M. Nana Mbianda, Maurice Boda, Sara Honorine Riwom Essama
Original abstract (English)
Background: Immune dysregulation is hypothesised to underpin the heightened susceptibility of HIV-1-exposed uninfected (HEU) infants to morbidity and mortality, which occurs at a rate three times higher than that of HIV-1-unexposed uninfected (HUU) controls. However, robust evidence substantiating this hypothesis and advocating targeted interventions remains scarce in Cameroon. Objective: To examine how maternal HIV-1 infection influences the immune response of HEU neonates in Yaoundé. Methods: A comparative cross-sectional study was conducted in 280 mother–child pairs at birth in Yaoundé. Clinical parameters were assessed, while umbilical cord blood samples from both HEU and HUU newborns were analysed for comprehensive immunophenotyping and quantification of 12 pro-inflammatory and anti-inflammatory cytokines. Results: Significant differences were observed between HEU (representing 100% of exposed newborns tested) and HUU newborns. Notably, 1/54 (1.85%) of HEU newborns exhibited a lower Appearance, Pulse, Grimace, Activity, Respiration score at 1 min (p = 0.01); HEU expressed severe leucocytosis (p = 0.008) and neutropaenia (p = 0.01). As well, dysregulated cytokine production was observed, with significantly elevated levels of pro-inflammatory cytokines Interleukin-6 (IL-6) (p = 0.003) and Interleukin-1 beta (IL-1β) (p = 0.004), as well as decreased levels of anti-inflammatory cytokine Transforming Growth Factor beta 1 (p = 0.024). Strong negative correlations were further demonstrated between monocytes and pro-inflammatory cytokines IL-1β (r = −0.74), Interleukin-5 (r = −0.75), and IL-6 (r = −0.75), (p < 0.001). Conclusion: Maternal HIV-1 infection is associated with impaired outcomes in HEU newborns, highlighted by immune dysregulation in an early pro-inflammatory environment. These findings underscore the heightened vulnerability of HEU neonates and elucidates the underlying factors contributing to their subsequent health challenges. What this study adds: It provides initial information on pertinent but not yet described pro-inflammatory cytokines impairment in Cameroonian HEU newborns. Unlike previous research that has focused on their KIR-21 gene expression and responsiveness to measles vaccine, this study introduces their immunological responses through their umbilical cord blood analysis, a previously unexamined specimen.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
However, robust evidence substantiating this hypothesis and advocating targeted interventions remains scarce in Cameroon.
Design and population
A comparative cross-sectional study was conducted in 280 mother–child pairs at birth in Yaoundé.
Core methods
Clinical parameters were assessed, while umbilical cord blood samples from both HEU and HUU newborns were analysed for comprehensive immunophenotyping and quantification of 12 pro-inflammatory and anti-inflammatory cytokines.
Main results
As well, dysregulated cytokine production was observed, with significantly elevated levels of pro-inflammatory cytokines Interleukin-6 (IL-6) (p = 0.003) and Interleukin-1 beta (IL-1β) (p = 0.004), as well as decreased levels of anti-inflammatory cytokine Transforming Growth Factor beta 1 (p = 0.024).
Interpretation and novelty
These findings underscore the heightened vulnerability of HEU neonates and elucidates the underlying factors contributing to their subsequent health challenges.

Limitations and evidence strength
